MoQ nightly benchmarks

Nightly 9387174a0, 221 flagged of 3919 series. Each series compares its newest point with the median of its previous 7. Red is worse and green is better by more than 10% and 3 times that window's median absolute deviation. Grey rows lack history or come from a source that did not finish tonight. Metrics and local reproduction

Paired comparison

Each source built the previous nightly's commit beside this one and ran both on one runner, alternating which went first.
